assert: Only the four front vertices are selectable; the rear corners remain occluded
assert: The first two visible vertices share the top boundary of the front quad
assert: Every tutorial vertex is directly clickable outside the gizmo overlay
assert: The edited object's boundary feedback is exercised with the general edge display switched off
assert: The display menu closes before clicking any vertex
assert: The first plain click selects exactly its visible vertex (selected={8}, target=8)
assert: A click outside the small painted marker still picks the nearby vertex
assert: Selecting two adjacent front vertices produces one solid selected boundary and four endpoint gradients, with no face tint or triangulation diagonal (selected={7, 8}, expected={7, 8}, counts=FeedbackCounts { solid_edges: 1, gradient_edges: 4, dark_edges: 7, selected_faces: 0, selected_triangles: 0 })
assert: Three selected corners cannot tint either derived triangle of the original four-corner polygon
assert: Selecting all four front vertices highlights exactly that original quad and its two render triangles, preserving dark rear boundaries and shared edge gradients
assert: Right-drag orbits the fully selected front face into an oblique view without altering its vertex, edge, or original-face selection
assert: Shift-clicking one selected corner removes its selection and immediately clears the face tint
assert: Selection feedback changes no authored geometry or mesh revision
assert: Leaving vertex editing clears the component-rendering payload while retaining the object and geometry
assert: Changing the selection and its colors creates no geometry undo entry

assert: Changing numeric movement from X to Y reuses the amount against the original baseline
assert: Toggling the numeric axis off restores the baseline while leaving the session explicitly finishable
assert: Cancelling an already-restored numeric session adds no geometry change
assert: All numeric tutorial previews leave the original object scale after undo or cancel
